Batas ukuran 5MB\n            if (file.size \u003e 5 * 1024 * 1024) {\n                showToast(\u0027Ukuran foto maksimal adalah 5 MB.\u0027, \u0027error\u0027);\n                return;\n             const img \u003d new Image();\n                img.onload \u003d function() {\n                    const canvas \u003d document.createElement(\u0027canvas\u0027);\n                    const maxDim \u003d 900;\n                    let width \u003d img.width;\n                    let height \u003d img.height;\n\n                    if (width \u003e maxDim || height \u003e maxDim) {\n                        if (width \u003e height) {\n                            height \u003d Math.round((height * maxDim) / width);\n                            width \u003d maxDim;\n                        } else {\n                            width \u003d Math.round((width * maxDim) / height);\n                            height \u003d maxDim;\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    canvas.width \u003d width;\n                    canvas.height \u003d height;\n                    const ctx \u003d canvas.getContext(\u00272d\u0027);\n                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);\n\n                    uploadedImageBase64 \u003d canvas.toDataURL(\u0027image/jpeg\u0027, 0.8);\n                    uploadedFileName \u003d file.name;\n\n                    // Tampilkan pratinjau\n                    document.getElementById(\u0027imagePreviewEl\u0027).src \u003d uploadedImageBase64;\n document.getElementById(\u0027fotoErrorMsg\u0027).classList.add(\u0027hidden\u0027);\n                };\n                img.src \u003d e.target.result;\n            };\n
